### 2306.05100 / midloop / alg-001

**检查内容：** The proximal operator of R is defined as prox\_{γR}(x) = (x̄, …, x̄) where x̄ = (1/n) ∑\_{i=1}^n x\_i (see Section 2.1).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.399%）。

**论文定位：** Section 2.1, after (3)。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:8)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:35)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In proxskip\_vip/algorithms.py, lines 90-95 (inside `proxskip_fl`): `candidates = xhat - (gamma / probability) * h` computes a per-client vector, then `x_new = np.mean(candidates, axis=0)` (line 91) computes the mean across all clients, and `x_clients[:] = x_new` (line 95) assigns this same mean vector to all clients. The same pattern appears in `proxskip_l_svrgda_fl` at line 151. This directly implements prox\_{γR}(x) = (x̄, …, x̄) where x̄ = (1/n) ∑\_{i=1}^n x\_i.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The proximal operator is applied to the shifted candidates rather than x directly (as expected from the ProxSkip algorithm structure), but the mean-then-broadcast operation correctly implements the proximal operator of R.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:35)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/algorithms.py, the proximal operator of R is implemented in the proxskip function (lines 50–51): `x = xdash.mean(axis=0); clients[:] = x`. This sets all n clients to the mean of their current values, i.e., (x̄, …, x̄) where x̄ = (1/n)∑\_i xdash\[i\]. The same pattern appears in proxskip\_l\_svrgda (lines 96–97) and matrix\_proxskip (line 218–219). The communication step in all ProxSkip variants applies this averaging (mean) and broadcasts the result to all clients.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> There is no standalone function named `prox_R`; the proximal operator is inlined directly into the communication step of each algorithm. The mathematical equivalence is exact: setting all clients to the mean is precisely prox\_{γR}(x) = (x̄, …, x̄) for any γ \> 0, since R = 0 if all x\_i equal and ∞ otherwise (indicator of consensus), or a quadratic consensus penalty.

### 2306.05100 / midloop / alg-002

**检查内容：** Algorithm 1: x̃\_{t+1} = x\_t - γ (g\_t - h\_t) (line 2).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.399%）。

**论文定位：** Alg.1 line 2。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:17)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:46)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> proxskip\_vip/algorithms.py, in the `proxskip_fl` function (~lines 82-87): `g = operator(x_clients[i], i, indices[i])` followed by `xhat[i] = x_clients[i] - gamma * (g - h[i])`. This directly implements x̃\_{t+1} = x\_t - γ(g\_t - h\_t), where xhat\[i\] = x̃\_{t+1}, x\_clients\[i\] = x\_t, gamma = γ, g = g\_t, h\[i\] = h\_t.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The same pattern also appears in `proxskip_l_svrgda_fl` with a variance-reduced gradient estimate.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:46)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/algorithms.py, the update rule x̃\_{t+1} = x\_t - γ(g\_t - h\_t) is implemented in multiple places: (1) proxskip() line ~44: `xhat[i] = clients[i] - gamma * (gradient_scale * g - h[i])`, where xhat=x̃, clients\[i\]=x\_t, g=g\_t, h\[i\]=h\_t, gamma=γ; (2) matrix\_proxskip() ~line 122: `xhat[i] = clients[i] - gamma * (game.operator(clients[i], i) - h[i])`; (3) proxskip\_l\_svrgda() uses the same pattern with a variance-reduced estimator.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The variable `xhat` in the code corresponds to x̃\_{t+1} in Algorithm 1. The formula matches exactly.

The branch then controls whether a communication/synchronization step occurs, matching Algorithm 1 line 3 exactly.

The result gates the communication/averaging block at lines 49–54, exactly matching Algorithm 1 line 3 semantics.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The variable is named `do_comm` rather than `theta_t`, but the logic is a direct Bernoulli(p) draw used as described.

### 2306.05100 / midloop / alg-004

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.399%）。

**论文定位：** Alg.1 lines 4-7。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:35)

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:68)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> The repository implements Algorithm 2 (proxskip\_fl, algorithms.py line 49) and Algorithm 4 (proxskip\_l\_svrgda\_fl, line 104), but no standalone Algorithm 1. The docstrings explicitly label these as "Algorithm 2" and "Algorithm 4". The FL variant's communication step uses client averaging (np.mean) rather than the prox\_{γ/p R}(·) proximal operator step specified in Algorithm 1 lines 4-7. No function implementing single-machine ProxSkip with an explicit proximal operator on regularizer R was found anywhere in the codebase.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> Algorithm 1 in the paper (2306.05100) is the base single-machine ProxSkip-VIP with a proximal operator. The FL version (Algorithm 2) shares the structural if/else branching but replaces prox with a client-averaging step, making it a different algorithm. The rubric's claim about Algorithm 1 with prox\_{γ/p R} is not implemented.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:68)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/algorithms.py, the proxskip() function (line 16) implements the update but never calls any proximal operator. When do\_comm=True (θ\_t=1), the code does: xdash\[i\] = xhat\[i\] - (gamma/probability)\*h\[i\] (line 44), then x = xdash.mean(axis=0) (line 47). The prox\_{γ/p R}(·) step is entirely missing — only averaging across clients is performed. No prox function is defined or called anywhere in the codebase (grep confirms 'prox' only appears as part of function names like 'proxskip', never as a proximal operator computation).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The algorithm being implemented (ProxSkip-GDA-FL, labeled Algorithm 2 in the file's docstring) may be a different algorithm than Algorithm 1 from the paper. Algorithm 1 may be the base ProxSkip with regularizer R requiring an explicit prox computation, which is absent here. The code's proxskip is for a federated VIP setting where the 'prox' step would correspond to the aggregation/projection, but no explicit prox operator is applied.

### 2306.05100 / midloop / alg-005

**检查内容：** Algorithm 1: h\_{t+1} = h\_t + (γ/p)(x\_{t+1} - x̃\_{t+1}) (line 9).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.399%）。

**论文定位：** Alg.1 line 9。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:44)

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:79)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In proxskip\_vip/algorithms.py, the h update in proxskip\_fl (line ~93) is: `h += (probability / gamma) * (x_new[None, :] - xhat)`, which corresponds to h\_{t+1} = h\_t + (p/γ)(x\_{t+1} - x̃\_{t+1}). The rubric claims the coefficient is (γ/p), but the code uses (p/γ) — the reciprocal. The same (p/γ) pattern appears in proxskip\_l\_svrgda\_fl as well.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code may be correct per the paper's actual formula; the rubric may have the coefficient inverted. The structure of the update (h += scalar \* (x\_new - xhat)) matches line 9 of Alg. 1 in form, but the scalar is p/γ not γ/p as claimed.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:79)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/algorithms.py, all h-update lines use the coefficient (probability/gamma) = p/γ: line 52 `h += (probability / gamma) * (clients - xhat)`, line 98 `h += (communication_probability / gamma) * (clients - xhat)`, and line 220 `h += probability / gamma * (clients - xhat)`. The rubric claims the update should be h\_{t+1} = h\_t + (γ/p)(x\_{t+1} - x̃\_{t+1}), which requires a coefficient of γ/p — the inverse of what is implemented.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The variable mapping is: `clients` → x\_{t+1}, `xhat` → x̃\_{t+1}. The direction of the update (addition, same difference operands) matches, but the scalar coefficient is inverted: code uses p/γ, rubric claims γ/p.

Lines ~65–70 show: `j = int(rng.integers(problem.n_components))` (uniform sample from \[n\]) followed by `estimator = (problem.component(clients[i], i, j) - problem.component(snapshots[i], i, j) + problem.client(snapshots[i], i))`. This is exactly `F_{j_t}(x_t) - F_{j_t}(w_t) + F(w_t)` where `snapshots[i]` is `w_t`. The `snapshot_probability` parameter (q) governs snapshot refresh (`if rng.random() < snapshot_probability: snapshots[i] = clients[i]`), matching the loopless-SVRG structure of Algorithm 3.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The rubric says "j\_t is sampled uniformly from \[n\] with probability q" — in the code, j\_t is always sampled uniformly (probability 1), while q controls snapshot refresh. This is the standard loopless-SVRG interpretation: j\_t is unconditionally drawn each step, and q is the snapshot update probability. The formula construction itself is fully and correctly implemented.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In proxskip\_vip/algorithms.py, the `proxskip_fl` function (lines 49-101) implements ProxSkip-GDA. At line 83, indices are computed as: `indices = [int(rng.integers(samples[i])) if stochastic and samples else None for i in range(clients)]`. In the deterministic case (`stochastic=False`), `indices[i]` is always `None`. At line 86, `g = operator(x_clients[i], i, indices[i])` thus calls `operator(x_clients[i], i, None)`, which by convention (also confirmed in `proxskip_l_svrgda_fl` at line 143 where `None` means full operator) is the exact operator F(x\_t). The update at line 87 then uses this as `xhat[i] = x_clients[i] - gamma * (g - h[i])`, matching ProxSkip-GDA with g\_t = F(x\_t).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The `None` index signals the full/exact operator evaluation, as confirmed by its use in `proxskip_l_svrgda_fl` at line 143 where `operator(snapshots[i], i, None)` is the full-batch snapshot gradient. The deterministic path in `proxskip_fl` indeed uses the exact operator.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:277)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `src/vip_repro/algorithms.py`, the `proxskip` function (lines ~35-55) computes the estimator `g` with a branch on `stochastic`: when `stochastic=False` (the deterministic ProxSkip-GDA case), it uses `g = problem.client(clients[i], i)`, which is the full/exact client operator evaluated at the current iterate `clients[i]` — i.e., the exact operator F(x\_t). In the stochastic case it instead uses a random component via `problem.component(clients[i], i, rng.integers(problem.n_components))`.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The `problem.client` method returns the exact (full) operator for that client, confirming this is F(x\_t) exactly, not an approximation.

Since \_psd\_matrix draws fresh random values from the RNG each call, A\_{ij}, B\_{ij}, C\_{ij} (and the offset components a\_{ij}, c\_{ij}) differ across every client-component pair, making the data heterogeneous across clients.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper uses a\_{ij}, c\_{ij} as offset/linear terms; in the code these are packed into the offsets array (size 2d, one half per player). The heterogeneity is guaranteed by the sequential RNG calls inside the nested loop.

Thus γ = 1/(2 × max\_i ℓ\_i) exactly as stated in the claim.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/cli.py (lines 42-43): gamma = 1.0 / (2.0 \* problem.ell) and probability = float(np.sqrt(problem.mu / (2.0 \* problem.ell))). **理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code expresses p as sqrt(mu/(2\*ell)) rather than sqrt(gamma\*mu) directly, but these are algebraically equivalent given gamma=1/(2\*ell).

This exactly implements p = √(γμ) where γ is the stochastic step size and μ is the strong monotonicity constant.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

Since `gamma = 1/(2*ell)`, this means `probability = sqrt(gamma * mu) = sqrt(γμ)`. The config\_resolved.json for the quadratic\_stochastic experiment confirms this: seed 0 shows gamma=0.10281, probability=0.21224, mu=0.43814 — and sqrt(0.10281 \* 0.43814) ≈ 0.2122.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code uses `ell` for the cocoercivity/smoothness constant (L). The formula p = sqrt(μ / (2L)) is algebraically equivalent to p = sqrt(γμ) since γ = 1/(2L).

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In proxskip\_vip/games.py lines 88-92, `relative_error(x)` computes `‖x - x*‖² / ‖x*‖²`. In proxskip\_vip/experiments.py line 142-143, x0 is initialized as `np.zeros(game.dimension)` so `‖x₀ - x*‖² = ‖x*‖²`, making the two formulas identical. The results files (e.g., results/formal/rls\_california/metrics.json) confirm the first metric value is always 1.0 (at k=0), consistent with ‖x₀ - x\*‖²/‖x₀ - x\*‖² = 1. The metric is applied to the rls\_california experiment via `metric = game.relative_error` (experiments.py line 143) and passed to all method calls (lines 156-161).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code literally normalizes by ‖x\*‖² rather than ‖x₀-x\*‖², but since x₀=0 in all experiments, these are numerically identical. The implementation is equivalent to the rubric's formula.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:508)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/problems.py lines 101-106, the `relative_error` method computes: `float(np.sum((x - self.optimum) ** 2) / denominator)` where `denominator = float(np.sum(self.optimum**2))`. This is ‖x - x\*‖² / ‖x\*‖² (normalizing by the squared norm of the optimum), NOT ‖x\_k - x\*‖² / ‖x\_0 - x\*‖² (normalizing by the initial error). The denominator is `self.optimum**2`, not the initial iterate's distance to the optimum.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The metric used is relative to the norm of the solution x\*, not relative to the initial error ‖x\_0 - x\*‖². The claim specifies the denominator should be ‖x\_0 - x\*‖² (initial error), but the code uses ‖x\*‖² instead.

So `problem.ell = max_i ell_i`. Thus γ = 1/(2·max\_i ℓ\_i) and p = sqrt(µ/(2·max\_i ℓ\_i)) = sqrt(γµ), exactly matching the rubric's formulas.

The configs/rls\_california.json note explicitly omits a stepsize\_rule field (unlike the quadratic configs), but the code directly computes all parameters from the problem's L/mu/ell constants in the same pattern as the quadratic experiments which are annotated with "gamma=1/(2\*ell), p=sqrt(mu/(2\*ell)); local EG gamma=1/(21\*K\*L)".

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

### 2306.05100 / midloop / setup-033

**检查内容：** The evaluation metric is the relative error ∥x\_k - x\*∥^2 / ∥x\_0 - x\*∥^2.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.399%）。

**论文定位：** Section 5, Appendix G.1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:764)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp\_robust\_ls\_synthetic；数据集=synthetic；方法=all；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp_robust_ls_synthetic",
    "method": "all",
    "dataset": "synthetic",
    "variant": ""
  }
}
```

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:640)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In proxskip\_vip/games.py lines 88-92, the `relative_error` method computes `‖x - x*‖² / ‖x*‖²` (denominator is `np.dot(self.solution, self.solution)`, i.e. ‖x\*‖²). The `run_rls` function in proxskip\_vip/experiments.py uses `metric = game.relative_error` for the rls\_synthetic experiment. The claimed metric ‖x\_k - x\*‖² / ‖x\_0 - x\*‖² would require the denominator to be the squared initial error ‖x\_0 - x\*‖², but instead the code normalizes by ‖x\*‖².

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The normalization differs from the claim: the code uses the squared norm of the solution x\* as denominator, not the squared initial error ‖x\_0 - x\*‖². When x\_0 = 0 (which it is in run\_rls), ‖x\_0 - x\*‖² = ‖x\*‖², so the two formulas would be equivalent in that case.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:640)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `src/vip_repro/problems.py` lines 101-105, `relative_error(x)` computes `np.sum((x - self.optimum)**2) / np.sum(self.optimum**2)`. Since `self.optimum` is x\*, this is ‖x\_k - x\*‖² / ‖x\*‖². The initial state in `src/vip_repro/algorithms.py` line 13 is `np.zeros(problem.dimension)` when x0 is None (the default throughout all algorithm calls). Thus `x_0 = 0`, so `‖x_0 - x*‖² = ‖0 - x*‖² = ‖x*‖²`, making the denominator exactly ‖x\_0 - x\*‖². The metric is correctly ‖x\_k - x\*‖² / ‖x\_0 - x\*‖².

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The denominator is stored as ‖x\*‖² rather than ‖x\_0 - x\*‖² explicitly, but they are equivalent because x\_0 is always initialized to the zero vector.

This gives F\_1(x) = x - \[delta, 0\] and F\_2(x) = x - \[0, delta\], so x\_1\*=\[delta,0\], x\_2\*=\[0,delta\], and x\*=(1/2)(x\_1\*+x\_2\*)=\[delta/2, delta/2\]. The global operator F=(1/2)(F\_1+F\_2) has solution at x\*. The `run_heterogeneity` function (lines ~286-304) uses exactly 2 clients and its metadata explicitly says "two-client varying heterogeneity game, Appendix H.4". The experiment\_id "heterogeneity" maps to this runner.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

This means F\_1(x) = x - x\_1\* where x\_1\* = \[delta,0\], and F\_2(x) = x - x\_2\* where x\_2\* = \[0,delta\]. The AffineVIP class averages client operators to form the global operator F(x) = (1/2)(F\_1(x)+F\_2(x)), and computes the optimum as x\* = -global\_matrix^{-1}\*global\_offset = \[delta/2, delta/2\] = (1/2)(x\_1\*+x\_2\*). The experiment is run via `heterogeneity_low` (delta=0.0) and `heterogeneity_high` (delta=1e6).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The delta parameter controls heterogeneity: delta=0 gives x\_1\*=x\_2\* (low heterogeneity) and delta=1e6 gives strongly differing local optima (high heterogeneity).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The number of clients n corresponds to the finite-sum index; the operator used in the experiment is the per-client operator (one sample per client), meaning the finite sum is over clients. The simplex dimension is (row-1)-dimensional and (col-1)-dimensional respectively.

The operator at line 281-283 implements the gradient of x^⊤A\_iy w.r.t. (x,y). The `project()` method (lines 285-286) projects both x and y components onto the standard simplex via `_project_simplex()` (lines 294-302), which implements the standard simplex projection. The `duality_gap()` (lines 288-291) computes max\_y x^⊤Ay - min\_x x^⊤Ay over both simplices, confirming the min\_x max\_y x^⊤(1/n ΣA\_i)y formulation over Δ^{d-1}×Δ^{d-1}.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> proxskip\_vip/games.py lines 253-258: MatrixGame.dual\_gap(x) computes max\_player = np.max(x1 @ a) (i.e., max\_{y∈Δ} f(x̂, y)) and min\_player = np.min(a @ x2) (i.e., min\_{x∈Δ} f(x, ŷ)), returning max\_player - min\_player. The class is explicitly labeled "Constrained Policemen-Burglar matrix game (Appendix H.5)". In proxskip\_vip/experiments.py, run\_matrix() (line 174-201) uses game.dual\_gap as the metric (lines 188, 195) and returns ylabel="Duality gap" (line 201).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The iterates are time-averaged (running/count) before being passed to dual\_gap for ProxSkip-GDA-FL and Local GDA, while Local EG uses dual\_gap directly. The formula itself matches the rubric exactly.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> src/vip\_repro/problems.py lines 288-291 implements duality\_gap as: max(A.T @ x\_hat) - min(A @ y\_hat), i.e. max\_{y∈Δ} f(x̂,y) - min\_{x∈Δ} f(x,ŷ). src/vip\_repro/cli.py line 204 sets metric\_name="duality\_gap" for the matrix\_game (Policemen-Burglar) experiment. src/vip\_repro/algorithms.py (lines 207, 223, 235, 242, 243, 253, 261, 262) records game.duality\_gap(x) as the metric throughout all algorithm runs.

### 2306.05100 / midloop / setup-059

**检查内容：** The duality gap is evaluated on the moving average of the iterates: (1/(K+1)) Σ\_{k=0}^K x\_k, where x\_k is the output after k communication rounds.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 1.399%）。

**论文定位：** Appendix H.5。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:1215)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp\_policemen\_burglar；数据集=Policemen Burglar；方法=all；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp_policemen_burglar",
    "method": "all",
    "dataset": "Policemen Burglar"
  }
}
```

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:926)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In proxskip\_vip/experiments.py, the `run_matrix` function (Policemen-Burglar experiment, Appendix H.5) defines a `moving_metric()` closure that accumulates a running sum of iterates and evaluates the duality gap on `running / count`. Concretely: `running = running + np.asarray(x); count += 1; return game.dual_gap(running / count)`. This is applied to ProxSkip-GDA-FL and Local GDA methods (Local EG uses the raw dual\_gap directly). The pattern matches (1/(K+1)) Σ\_{k=0}^K x\_k exactly.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The moving average metric is used for ProxSkip-GDA-FL and Local GDA, but Local EG uses `game.dual_gap` directly (not the moving average). The rubric says "all" methods, but the primary claim about evaluating duality gap on the moving average is clearly implemented for the main methods in the Policemen-Burglar experiment.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:926)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In src/vip\_repro/algorithms.py, the `matrix_proxskip` function (the algorithm for the Policemen Burglar game) explicitly computes the duality gap on the moving average of iterates. After each communication round: `x_sum += x` accumulates the sum of iterates, and `errors.append(game.duality_gap(x_sum / comm))` evaluates the duality gap on the average `(1/comm) * Σ_{k=1}^{comm} x_k`. The same pattern is used in `matrix_local_gda` and `matrix_local_eg`. This directly implements the claim that duality gap is evaluated on the moving average of the iterates.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper's formula is `(1/(K+1)) Σ_{k=0}^K x_k` (0-indexed, includes x\_0), while the code uses `(1/K) Σ_{k=1}^K x_k` (1-indexed, excludes the initial point). This is a minor notational difference; the key claim that duality gap is evaluated on the running/moving average of iterates is clearly and concretely implemented.

### 2306.05100 / bigloop / res-001

**检查内容：** ProxSkip-GDA-FL is faster than Local GDA in terms of communication rounds on the deterministic quadratic game.

**类别与权重：** `exp_results`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 6.061%）。

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> results/formal/quadratic/metrics.json: ProxSkip-GDA-FL achieves relative\_error=5.33e-06 by round 15 and 2.69e-31 by round 400. Local GDA never converges—its relative\_error remains ~0.48 (essentially starting level) after all 400 communication rounds. ProxSkip-GDA-FL clearly has a far lower relative error at every comparable round, establishing it as strictly faster in terms of communication rounds to convergence on the deterministic quadratic game.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> Local GDA appears to not converge at all (diverges or oscillates near initial error), while ProxSkip-GDA-FL converges to machine precision within ~40 rounds. The ranking claim is trivially satisfied.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> results/formal/quadratic\_deterministic/metrics.csv and summary.json: ProxSkip-GDA-FL reaches relative\_error \< 1e-3 at round 7 and converges to ~3.2e-31 by round 400 (final\_mean=2.36e-31). Local GDA never drops below 1e-3 across all 400 communication rounds, finishing at relative\_error ≈ 0.483 (final\_mean=0.481). ProxSkip-GDA-FL is unambiguously faster (lower relative\_error at every round) than Local GDA on the deterministic quadratic game.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The metric column in the CSV is relative\_error per round. The "round" column appears to be the communication round index. Local GDA does not converge within 400 rounds while ProxSkip-GDA-FL converges well within 10 rounds.

### 2306.05100 / bigloop / res-004

**论文定位：** Section 5。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_05100/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_bigloop.json:86)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp\_quadratic\_game；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {

  }
}
```

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:63)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> results/formal/quadratic/metrics.json and results/formal/quadratic/run.log confirm ProxSkip-L-SVRGDA-FL runs for 401 iterations on the quadratic game. The error sequence decreases monotonically (400/400 steps decreasing) from 1.0 to 8.09e-09. A log-linear regression of the error sequence yields slope = -0.020112 log10-units per iteration with R² = 0.9995, indicating near-perfect linear (geometric) convergence. The run.log also reports: ProxSkip-L-SVRGDA-FL: final=8.09258894e-09. The method converges to a near-zero residual (~1e-8), consistent with convergence to the exact solution.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The final error is ~8e-9 (not exactly 0), but this is consistent with floating-point precision limits and the run was terminated at 401 iterations. The R²=0.9995 of the log-linear fit strongly supports linear (geometric) convergence.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`medium`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:63)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> ProxSkip-L-SVRGDA-FL appears only in results/formal/quadratic\_stochastic/metrics.csv (not in quadratic\_deterministic). Its relative\_error trajectory is approximately log-linear (slope=-0.00864/round, R²=0.9986), consistent with linear convergence rate. However, after 400 communication rounds the metric stagnates at ~0.031–0.041 (final\_mean=0.0363 per summary.json), never approaching zero. The claim requires convergence "to the exact solution," but the outputs show convergence to a neighborhood (~3–4% relative error), not to zero. The deterministic quadratic experiment (which does reach near-zero error ~1e-31) does not include ProxSkip-L-SVRGDA-FL at all — only ProxSkip-GDA-FL is tested there. No run output demonstrates ProxSkip-L-SVRGDA-FL converging to the exact solution on the quadratic game.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The SVRG variance-reduction is theoretically meant to enable exact convergence in stochastic settings, and the convergence rate appears linear. However, the actual run data only runs 400 rounds with final error ~3-4%, which is ambiguous: it could be that more rounds would reach zero, but the run outputs do not demonstrate this. The verdict is 0 because the outputs do not show convergence to the exact solution within the experiment's recorded horizon.

The claim requires comparable performance among SProxSkip, Local SGDA, and Local SEG; the outputs show roughly an order-of-magnitude gap between Local SEG (~0.08) and Local SGDA (~0.76), with SProxSkip in between (~0.27). This does not constitute comparable performance.

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.05100/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:217)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> Two heterogeneity experiments exist: heterogeneity\_low (δ=0) and heterogeneity\_high (δ=1e6), confirmed by config\_resolved.json. The outputs contain a method called 'ProxSkip-GDA-FL', not 'ProxSkip-VIP-FL' — no method by that exact name appears anywhere in any output file. For δ=0 (heterogeneity\_low/metrics.csv): ALL methods (ProxSkip-GDA-FL, Local GDA, Local EG) report metric=0.0 at every round for all 3 seeds — all methods are tied at zero relative error, so ProxSkip-VIP-FL cannot be shown to outperform the baselines. For δ=1e6 (heterogeneity\_high/summary.json): ProxSkip-GDA-FL final\_mean≈2.0e-08 vs Local EG final\_mean=0.3876 vs Local GDA final\_mean=0.9252 — ProxSkip-GDA-FL clearly outperforms both. The claim fails on two counts: (1) method name 'ProxSkip-VIP-FL' is absent from all run outputs; (2) for δ=0 the ranking cannot be established as all methods are tied at 0.0.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The 'ProxSkip-GDA-FL' method in the outputs may correspond to 'ProxSkip-VIP-FL' in the paper (VIP-FL being the general variational inequality framework). Even if we accept this identification, the δ=0 case shows all methods at metric=0.0 throughout all rounds, so a strict ranking of ProxSkip outperforming Local GDA and Local EG cannot be established from these outputs — at best they are equal. Only the δ=1e6 case clearly supports the ranking claim.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> Run output found at ./results/formal/matrix/metrics.json (confirmed as "Policemen-Burglar matrix game, Appendix H.5" via ./results/formal/matrix/run\_metadata.json). Final duality gap values (at communication round 500): ProxSkip-GDA-FL=0.008883, Local EG=0.007861, Local GDA=0.194524. Minimum values across all rounds: ProxSkip-GDA-FL=0.008869, Local EG=0.004615 (at round 383), Local GDA=0.185562. The constraint 'ProxSkip-GDA-FL \> Local EG' (lower\_is\_better, so ProxSkip-GDA-FL must have lower duality gap) FAILS: Local EG achieves a lower duality gap (0.00786 final, 0.00462 min) than ProxSkip-GDA-FL (0.00888 final, 0.00887 min). The constraint 'ProxSkip-GDA-FL \> Local GDA' holds (0.00888 \<\< 0.1945). One of the two required ranking constraints is not satisfied.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The claim says ProxSkip-GDA-FL 'clearly outperforms' both methods. While it clearly beats Local GDA, Local EG actually achieves a lower (better) duality gap than ProxSkip-GDA-FL in the run outputs, contradicting the required ranking.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Final duality gap values (mean across seeds): ProxSkip-GDA-FL=0.004562, Local EG=0.030258, Local GDA=0.024993. Both ranking constraints hold: ProxSkip-GDA-FL \< Local EG (6.6x lower duality gap) and ProxSkip-GDA-FL \< Local GDA (5.5x lower duality gap). Lower is better, so ProxSkip-GDA-FL clearly outperforms both baselines.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The experiment directory is named "matrix\_game" rather than "policemen\_burglar", but the correspondence is confirmed via problem class docstring and CLAIMS.json descriptions.
